Forgetting, plasticity, and co-observation: a third facet of continual learning

Timm Hess, Abhishek Jha, Gido M. van de Ven, Tinne Tuytelaars

Continual learning performance is affected not only by forgetting and plasticity, but also by whether data are observed together during training.

Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective

Yuanyuan Xu, Wenjie Zhang, Yin Chen, Xuemin Lin, Ying Zhang

This survey reframes self-evolving LLM agents as dynamic graphs whose components and relationships change through schema-constrained transformations.
